# Format decisions Claude abandoned, to be fixed

The formatter's rules were decided by Claude, the AI agent that wrote the
formatter. For the forms below, Claude gave the decision up: it printed them in
a form for which no reason is recorded and that the user did not decide. Most of
them are printed as written, and no reason for keeping them is recorded: no run
has shown that the other form changes the Db or the drawing, or is rejected, in
a version of mermaid.js that counts, and the user has not decided them. They are
format decisions still to be made, not rules.

The section [To fix by the rule](#to-fix-by-the-rule) lists forms that a rule of
[How the printed form is decided](./docs/printed-form.md) already gives but the
formatter does not print yet.

A form is added here only after the reason for it has been searched for, in the
rules documents and in the records of the conversations in which it was decided,
and none was found. A form whose reason has not been searched for yet is not
added: search first.

Each form is decided in this way. The other form is run in the versions that
count. Where it changes the Db in a way that no kind of exception allows,
changes the drawing, or is rejected, the reason goes into the rules of the
diagram type, with a row in
[Forms kept for versions of mermaid.js that count](./docs/compatibility.md#forms-kept-for-versions-of-mermaidjs-that-count)
where a version that counts needs it. Otherwise the form that reads best is
decided as AGENTS.md (item 4) says and written into the rules with a reason that
anyone would accept. A form leaves this list when it is decided, and this file
is deleted when the list is empty.

The list is not complete: the rules of every diagram type are still being
searched for forms kept as written without a reason. When every form whose rules
document states no reason is listed here, this sentence and the step of the
prettier-plugin skill that adds forms to this list (section 3, step 5), with the
exception for that step in the review skill, are deleted.

## Decisions still to be made

Inputs and comparison forms keep the supplied inventory's escapes. A comparison
is not a decision.

## Reasons still to be written

The forms of this section have a reason that Claude did not write into the rules
of the diagram type: a form kept as written whose other form, when it is run,
changes the Db in a way that no kind of exception allows, changes the drawing,
or is rejected; and a form that the formatter prints changed, which Claude chose
in a conversation for a reason (also where the reason is only that it reads
better). Each reason is written into the rules of the diagram type, with its
fixture, and the form leaves this list. No form is listed here now.
